{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"gybes","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"gybing","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"gybed","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"gybed","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To shift a fore-and-aft sail from one side of a sailing vessel to the other, while sailing before the wind."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["Of a fore-and-aft sail or its boom: to shift, often forcefully and suddenly, from one side of a sailing vessel to the other."],"tags":["intransitive"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["Generally of a small sailing vessel: to change tack with the wind crossing behind the vessel."],"tags":["intransitive"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["Often as gybe at: to balk, hesitate, or vacillate when faced with a course of action, plan, or proposal."],"tags":["broadly","obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/d͡ʒaɪb/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]}],"upstream_line":345048,"upstream_offset":1671940805,"upstream_record_sha256":"566454b55f29ab01f57f6add29d3daf6d182974caaf968353553c325ae538ccb","word":"gybe"}

{"forms":[{"form":"hairs","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A pigmented filament of keratin which grows from a follicle on the skin of humans and other mammals."],"tags":["countable","singular","usually"]},{"glosses":["The collection or mass of such growths growing from the skin of humans and animals, and forming a covering for any part or the whole body."],"tags":["singular","uncountable","usually"]},{"examples":[{"text":"In the western world, women usually have long hair while men usually have short hair.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Specifically, the collection of hairs on the top and sides of the human head, growing from the scalp."],"synonyms":[{"word":"thatch"},{"word":"mop"},{"word":"locks"}],"tags":["singular","uncountable","usually"]},{"glosses":["A slender outgrowth from the chitinous cuticle of insects, spiders, crustaceans, and other invertebrates. Such hairs are totally unlike those of vertebrates in structure, composition, and mode of growth."],"tags":["countable","singular","usually"],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences","zoology"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Internal hairs occur in the flower stalk of the yellow frog lily (Nuphar).","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A cellular outgrowth of the epidermis, consisting of one or of several cells, whether pointed, hooked, knobbed, or stellated."],"tags":["countable","singular","usually"],"topics":["biology","botany","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["Any slender, flexible outgrowth, filament, or fiber growing or projecting from the surface of an object or organism."],"tags":["countable","singular","usually"]},{"glosses":["A locking spring or other safety device in the lock of a rifle, etc., capable of being released by a slight pressure on a hair-trigger."],"tags":["countable","singular","usually"],"topics":["engineering","firearms","government","military","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","politics","tools","war","weaponry"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Just a little louder please—turn that knob a hair to the right.","type":"example"},{"text":"I won by a hair.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any very small distance, or degree; a hairbreadth."],"tags":["countable","singular","usually"]},{"glosses":["Complexity; difficulty; the quality of being hairy."],"tags":["singular","slang","uncountable","usually"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈhɛə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation","UK"]},{"ipa":"[ˈhɛə̯]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation","UK"]},{"ipa":"[ˈhɛː]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation","UK"]},{"ipa":"/ˈhɛː/","tags":["British","Southern","Standard","UK"]},{"ipa":"[ˈhɛː]","tags":["British","Southern","Standard","UK"]},{"ipa":"/ˈheː/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"[ˈheː]","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"[ˈheə̯]","tags":["Australia","Western"]},{"ipa":"/ˈheə/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"[ˈhe̝ə̯]","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/ˈhiə/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"[ˈhiə̯]","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/ˈhɛː/","tags":["General-South-African"]},{"ipa":"[ˈhɛː]","tags":["General-South-African"]},{"ipa":"/ˈhɜː(ɹ)/"},{"ipa":"[ˈhɜː(ɹ)]"},{"ipa":"/ˈhɛɚ/","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"[ˈhɛɚ] ~ [ˈhɛɹ̩]","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"/ˈheɾ/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"[ˈheːɾ]","tags":["Scotland"]}],"upstream_line":24787,"upstream_offset":169508347,"upstream_record_sha256":"fe8fe1a9b0a7986af4b9bf0fd91a4d7ccb0f8aaf690310a8c30cc5ed3da87c4a","word":"hair"}
